
`pcp_03_main_comparison.sbatch` (30 rows) and `pcp_04_ablations.sbatch` (201
rows) are written, tested, and **must not be submitted until the pilot is
read**. Their headers say so and explain why.

The pilot is two `pcp_comm_identity` rows, seed 0, at 60,000 and 120,000 frames,
under exactly the optimizer protocol `pcp_comm_main` uses (gamma 0.9, entropy
0.1, five minibatch iterations). It was corrected on 2026-09-03 to share that
protocol: it had inherited the Simple Spread pilot's pre-frozen settings
(gamma 0.99, entropy 0.0, ten iterations), which would have answered the budget
question for a configuration no PCP suite runs.
`test_pilot_shares_the_main_comparisons_optimizer_protocol` now pins this.

### How to read the pilot

```text
results/pcp_identity_pilot/REPORT.md
results/pcp_identity_pilot/pcp_identity_pilot_per_run.csv
runs/pcp_identity_pilot/<run_id>/metrics.csv
```

The reported return is the predators' own. Reference points measured locally on
this exact scenario:

| Quantity | Value | Source |
|---|---|---|
| adversary episode return, uniformly random policy | ~1.5 | direct 20-env rollout |
| adversary collection return, 30k-frame MLP row | 0.67 - 1.5, flat | local run |
| adversary evaluation return, deterministic, <= 60k frames | 0.0 at every point | local runs |
| prey return | exactly the negative of the predators' | simple_tag is zero-sum |

`metrics.csv` carries `return_mean` three times per point: ungrouped (the study
figure), `group=adversary`, and `group=agent`. The ungrouped row is the one the
analysis reads.

Saliency is exactly 0.0 for this pilot **by design** -- it runs
`pcp_comm_identity`, the no-communication control. That is the wiring-check
value, not a finding. Do not confuse it with the sparse-signal problem below.

### The decision the pilot exists to make

Compare the 60k and 120k rows on adversary return:

- **Rising, and above ~1.5**: the predators are learning. Pick a budget from the
  slope and launch `pcp_03`. A 600,000-frame row costs ~12 minutes on one core,
  so the whole 231-row study is ~45 core-hours; compute is not the constraint
  and never was.
- **Flat near ~1.5**: the policy is at chance. Do not launch 231 rows. The
  candidate causes, in the order worth testing, are below.
- **Deterministic evaluation still 0.0 while collection is non-zero**: the
  outcome metric cannot see the policy at all. Fix the measurement before
  spending any comparison rows -- a six-way comparison of zeros is worthless
  however many seeds it has.

### Open questions, in priority order

1. **Sparse capture reward.** `simple_tag` gives the adversaries `+10` only on
   contact (`shape_adversary_rew: false`). Enabling shaping gives a dense
   distance signal and is the standard remedy; it changes what the task rewards,
   so it is a study-design decision, not a fix to apply silently.
2. **Five evaluation episodes.** The evaluation environment is batched, so the
   episode count comes from `experiment.evaluation_episodes`, *not* from
   `scripts/saliency.py --episodes`. A rare-event outcome measured with five
   samples has almost no power. Raising it costs very little here.
3. **gamma 0.9 carried over untested.** ~10-step effective horizon against a
   sparse capture reward is a different proposition than against Simple Spread's
   dense coverage cost. The Simple Spread history (a three-run, 240k diagnostic
   isolating gamma and entropy) is the template if this needs its own answer.
4. **Budget never calibrated.** 60,000 frames is ten PPO update rounds and five
   evaluation points, so the final-10% statistic is a single point.

Whichever of these changes, update the suite YAMLs, rerun
`sbatch slurm/pcp_01_setup.sbatch` to rebuild the manifests, and re-read the
pilot before the main comparison. Also still deferred, deliberately:
role/class-id conditioning on PCP (no data source; see the section above).

## Pilot returned: PCP is blocked on the environment, not the config (2026-09-03)

`sbatch slurm/pcp_02_pilot.sbatch` ran as array 793465 on one V100. Both rows
completed. Full analysis in **`docs/RESULTS_pcp_pilot.md`**; read that before
touching any PCP sweep. Summary of what changed in this repo's understanding:

**The pilot's own question is answered.** Something does learn: collection
return rises 1.52 -> 2.50 across the 120k run (Welch p = 0.036) against a
measured random-policy baseline of 0.83, with slope +1.75 per 100k frames and
no plateau. Throughput is 255 frames/s including evaluation, so 600k-1M frames
is 39-65 min per identity row. Budget is not the binding constraint.

**Open question 4 is answered:** 120k frames is ~1/10 of what the task needs.
120k = 20 iterations x 10 minibatch epochs = 200 optimizer steps at lr 5e-5;
policy entropy moved 1.304 -> 1.146 and ESS stayed at 0.99 the whole run.

**Open question 3 is answered, and the answer is against `pcp_comm_main`.** The
prey's `max_speed` is 1.3 against the predators' 1.0, so a one-predator chase
never converges and capture needs a pincer. gamma = 0.9 discounts a reward 20
steps out to 0.12; gamma = 0.99 leaves it at 0.82. The pilot learned *because*
it accidentally ran gamma = 0.99 -- the alignment edit to
`configs/sweeps/pcp_identity_pilot.yaml` was never committed and the cluster
pulled `3bb11aa` without it, so the pilot ran gamma 0.99 / entropy 0.0 /
10 minibatch iters while `pcp_comm_main` still specifies 0.9 / 0.1 / 5.

**Open question 2 is quantified.** Over 300 random episodes: mean 0.833, std
3.789, and 94% of episodes score exactly zero. At the current
`evaluation_episodes: 5` the standard error is 1.69 -- larger than the whole
effect being measured. n=128 brings it to 0.34 and is nearly free: BenchMARL
passes `evaluation_episodes` to the eval env's `num_envs`
(`benchmarl/experiment/experiment.py:449`) and does one batched rollout
(`:932`), so a wider batch runs the same 100 sequential steps.

**A new blocker outranks all four.** `PredatorCapturePreyScenario` overrides
`process_action` only and inherits `observation()` from stock `simple_tag`, so
each predator's 16-dim observation already contains the prey's relative position
*and* velocity plus both teammates' positions. The task is **fully observable
with homogeneous predators** -- structurally the same condition that produced
the Simple Spread null. `docs/RESULTS_v2_simple_spread.md:400` justified moving
to PCP on the promise of partial observability and heterogeneous roles; neither
was ever implemented, and `README.md:174` / the section above already record the
role split as deferred. Running 231 ablation rows before fixing this reproduces
the Simple Spread null at higher cost.

**Fixed in this session.** `summarize_metrics_csv` in
`experiments/bookkeeping.py` averaged every evaluation `return_mean` row
regardless of group. PCP writes three per evaluation (the ungrouped study figure
plus one per group) and its groups are zero-sum, so `summary.json` reported
`x/3`: the 120k row's true `mean_final_return` is **7.00**, not the 2.33 it
printed. `analysis/aggregate.py` already filtered by group, so `results/` was
never affected and Simple Spread is bit-identical after the fix. Pinned by
`test_summary_ignores_per_group_returns_on_a_two_group_task`; 503 tests pass.

**Do not submit `pcp_03_main_comparison` or `pcp_04_ablations`.** Gates A and B
are now implemented, C is configured but not run, D is blocked on C.

**Gate A (done).** `PredatorCapturePreyScenario.observation` now honours a
`predator_sensing_radius`: past that distance a predator's view of the prey's
position *and* velocity is zeroed and a visibility flag is appended, taking the
predator observation to 17 dims. Predators still see each other, so the only
thing a message can carry that its receiver lacks is where the prey is -- and
which predators can answer that changes step to step, which is the dynamic
sender relevance Simple Spread could not pose. The masked block is located by
counting prey, not by a hardcoded index, and is verified bit-identical to the
stock observation where visible. `configs/tasks/vmas_predator_capture_prey.yaml`
sets **1.0**, calibrated against the random-policy geometry (mean distance
1.08): the team is blind 14% of steps, exactly one of three predators sees the
prey 41% of the time -- the modal case -- and all three see it only 10%. R = 0.6
was the first guess and is too harsh (nobody sees the prey 58% of the time, and
information no one holds cannot be communicated). The scenario default stays
`None`, so stock behaviour is still reachable and the pilot stays reproducible.
**Nothing measured before 2026-09-03 is comparable to a post-Gate-A row**,
including the 0.83 random baseline.

**Gate B (done).** `experiment.evaluation_episodes: 128` on all eight
`pcp_comm_*.yaml` and on the protocol gate. Measured, not assumed: 5 -> 128
episodes costs **+24% evaluation wall-clock for 25x the samples** (0.99s ->
1.23s), taking the standard error from 1.69 to 0.34. Pinned by a test.

**Gate C (configured, not run).** `configs/sweeps/pcp_protocol_gate.yaml` and
`slurm/pcp_02b_protocol.sbatch`: the control at gamma x entropy_coef, 3 x 3,
three seeds, 600k frames -- 27 rows, ~18 GPU-hours, under a tenth of the
comparison it protects. This supersedes `pcp_02_pilot`, whose question is
answered. Read off the winning cell *and* where its curve flattens; that frame
count, not 600k, is what the comparison sweeps should carry.

**Gate D (blocked).** All eight `pcp_comm_*.yaml` carry a `BLOCKED` header
naming the three placeholders the gate decides -- `max_n_frames` 60000, gamma
0.9, `entropy_coef` 0.1 -- and a test asserts the header is present. Take all
three from the winner, delete the header, rerun `pcp_01_setup`, then submit
step 3. Gate the 201 ablation rows on whether the 30-row main comparison
separates the mechanisms at all.

    sbatch slurm/pcp_01_setup.sbatch       # rebuilds manifests, incl. the gate
    sbatch slurm/pcp_02b_protocol.sbatch   # 27 rows  <- READ THIS FIRST

## Research assessment requested by user (2026-09-05)

The user requested an independent short assessment of feasibility, strengths,
weaknesses, and improvements after reading `../RESEARCH_OVERVIEW.md`. This was
an assessment task, not authorization to execute the overview's experiment
plan. Deliverable: **`../RESEARCH_ASSESSMENT.md`**, with local evidence links and
primary literature citations. The original overview was left unchanged. No
training jobs, training-path changes, or new experimental configurations were
made. Only this handoff and the new assessment were written.

Current observed repository state supersedes stale historical snapshots above:
`marl-comm` HEAD was `40a97a6` with only two pre-existing untracked `.DS_Store`
files before this handoff edit. The sibling `../MAPDN` was inspected read-only;
its HEAD was `e3808a0dfafaf118286226f7eb8e4b9a846e7f15`, with existing modified
adapter/environment files and untracked tests. The current `uv.lock` identifies
MAPDN revision `780b84c8a4237c92575dedb49fb3553b6e6804d6`. These observations
alone do not establish whether the sibling's dirty changes are present in that
locked revision.

New evidence and corrections, still **unfixed** in experimental code:

- Simple Spread is not a strict no-private-information control. Installed
  VMAS `mpe/simple_spread.py::observation` supplies own velocity but only
  teammates' positions. An isolated real-VMAS diagnostic changed one sender's
  velocity to `[0.6, -0.4]`: its observation changed while another agent's
  observation stayed bit-identical. This demonstrates private information;
  it does not establish that the learned messages actually encode velocity.
- `analysis/saliency.py::_behavioural_divergence` (line 207 onward) compares
  actions and distribution parameters across complete paired trajectories
  without checking state equality. Its reported action shift/KL are therefore
  not the same-state intervention described by the overview. Recompute both
  conditions on the same stored joint observations for that estimand. This
  finding alone does not invalidate the main training-return comparison.
- PCP's exact-pairing claim needs repair. `DropoutChannel(p=1)` still draws
  `torch.rand`; scripted prey noise/wander draws share that RNG. A direct
  CPU diagnostic held physical state, initial wander state, and RNG state
  fixed, and inserted just the `p=1` channel call before computing prey force:
  maximum action difference was `0.05460807681083679`. Also, setting
  `prey.wander_dir` to 0.5 and then seeding/resetting the environment retained
  0.5. The subclass initializes this state only in `make_world`, with no
  corresponding reset override. Isolate/replay exogenous randomness and reset
  all scripted state before claiming paired PCP counterfactuals. The diagnostic
  does not quantify bias in any saved estimate.
- PCP protocol gate YAML specifies 10 minibatch passes; the blocked main YAML
  specifies 5. Copying only gamma, entropy, and frame horizon after the gate
  would leave the comparison under an unvalidated protocol. Copy and verify
  the full scientific configuration. Installed BenchMARL's nested optimizer
  loops also show that the pilot's 20 collections x 10 passes x 15 minibatches
  imply 3,000 minibatch updates per trained group, not the documented 200.

Assessment recommendations (proposals, not newly frozen protocol decisions):
use within-PCP visibility manipulation as the primary causal experiment; retain
the same observation layout in both arms; separate trained-policy reliance from
task necessity; add an attainable privileged-information reference and a local
active-capacity control; estimate minimum tested communication budget against a
predeclared performance target. The existing `S/abs(J_severed)` fraction is not
invariant to return offsets, so do not use the ~6% Simple Spread number as a
universal cross-task threshold. Small effects under partial observability can
also be legitimate evidence, not necessarily a defective protocol. Self-only
use, gamma causation, and attention-selection explanations need direct evidence.

Literature checked: Lowe et al. (AAMAS 2019), including its explicit causal
interventions and test-time distribution-shift caution; Agarwal et al.
(NeurIPS 2021); I2C; CACL (ICLR 2024); the original MAPDN paper; and the DDCL
preprint arXiv:2511.01554 (2025). This was a focused check, not an exhaustive
novelty survey. MAPDN's BenchMARL binding remains a zero-line placeholder.

Verification: isolated observation/RNG/reset diagnostics as described above,
inspection of saved main-comparison CSVs, local Markdown link existence,
trailing-whitespace checks, and `git diff --check`. No full pytest/source gate
was rerun for these documentation-only changes. Two preliminary diagnostic
invocations needed harness corrections (VMAS requires the imported scenario
instance here, and `set_vel` requires `batch_index=None`); the final observation
diagnostic passed. Neither was a failure of a study run. No existing run/result
artifacts or sibling files were written. All research/code corrections above
remain pending for a separately authorized implementation task.
## Publication-strength follow-up (2026-09-05)

User asked what would strengthen the thesis for a poster at a top ML conference.
Advisory scope only; no implementation or experiments authorized/launched by
this question. Official ICML 2026 reviewer instructions explicitly recognize
original insights from evaluation of existing methods; NeurIPS 2026 Evaluations
and Datasets reviewer guidelines recognize systematic audits and carefully
controlled negative results. A main-conference paper presented as a poster and
a workshop contribution are separate submission targets, not equivalent claims
of acceptance. No acceptance prediction or fixed experiment-count guarantee.

Recommended research emphasis: a controlled within-task relationship between
information access and the minimum tested budget achieving a declared target;
separate information shared by all, informative observations held by some, and
information unavailable to the team as diagnostic conditions. Include active
local capacity, local memory, and privileged-information references; pair
frozen-policy severing with training under the budget and semantic perturbation
controls. Seek a finding beyond the expected fact that partial observability
can make communication useful, and replicate the central contrast in one
additional domain before broad claims. Resolve previously logged measurement
issues first. Primary precedents rechecked: I2C and DDCL (arXiv:2511.01554).
These are publication-design recommendations, not new frozen study settings.

unchanged. No training or new method implementation was requested or executed.

Four proposed directions, with experiments, effort, prior work, and novelty
limits recorded in the companion:

1. Select senders as a set, accounting for redundancy. Recommended algorithmic
   lead only after a bounded singleton/pair message-intervention diagnostic.
   A relevance-minus-similarity greedy selector is a hypothesis, not a novel
   algorithm claim; account for obtaining all preview keys and scheduling.
2. Study training-to-deployment transfer across independent, bursty, and
   spatially correlated failures at matched marginal failure rates and measured
   cost. Recommended empirical lead; domain recovery/tail outcomes matter.
3. MAPDN communication-graph prior misspecification at matched budgets, with
   offline sensitivity priors and controls for privileged online information.
4. Memory–bandwidth trade-offs. This would require a separate recurrent actor
   experiment with state/sequence/reset/replay validation if selected.

**Latest calibration evidence supersedes earlier pending-gate speculation:**
all 27 Identity protocol rows reached 600k, with 385,020 finite logged scalar
values and zero non-finite values. All record clean source
`40a97a612f160c80de3cf7a719fe4004cb33798e`, BenchMARL 1.5.2, TorchRL 0.11.1,
TensorDict 0.11.0, VMAS 1.5.2, Torch 2.13.0+cu126, Python 3.11.4, CUDA V100,
four threads. Configuration leaf comparison found only gamma, entropy, seed,
and output-path differences. Total recorded training time sums to 18.237 hours.

Final return / normalized AUC means across three seeds:

| Gamma | Entropy 0 | Entropy .01 | Entropy .1 |
|---|---|---|---|
| .90 | 5.438 / 5.219 | 8.628 / 6.504 | 615.317 / 186.737 |
| .95 | 5.313 / 4.081 | 119.431 / 44.566 | 668.381 / 303.249 |
| .99 | 213.433 / 79.346 | 345.760 / 169.318 | 586.823 / 329.832 |

Proposed candidate: gamma .95 / entropy .1, preserving **ten** PPO passes,
lr 5e-5, lambda .9, batch 6000, minibatch 400, ten training environments,
128 deterministic evaluation episodes, and the rest of the gate spec.
This is a recommendation, NOT a newly frozen protocol or changed config.
Gamma .99 leads AUC and .90 also learns strongly; remove unsupported claims
that only .99 can learn PCP. Final-return seed SDs at entropy .1 are 55.236,
34.447, 27.866 for gamma .90/.95/.99 respectively. Three calibration seeds do
not establish a unique optimum.

Correct metric interpretation: first evaluation is after training at **6000
frames**, not an untrained 0-frame evaluation. There are 51 evaluation points
and the final ceil(10%) window is **six**, 540k--600k, with 128 episodes each.
AUC spans 6k--600k. The audit reproduces every saved summary. Zero console
return is the framework's opposing-group aggregation, not failed learning;
the managed study return correctly selects adversary. PCP pays repeated +10
per predator-prey contact, defaults to no respawn, and does not end at first
contact. At entropy .1 all three gammas have about 91--92% final-window
episodes with any rewarded contact, despite different accumulated returns.
Domain metrics must separate capture probability/time and sustained contact.

Horizon remains a scientific choice: at .95/.1 mean return was 568.431 over
408k--492k evaluations and 659.523 over 504k--600k. 600k is a usable fixed
budget, not proven convergence. Recommend a bounded fresh longer-horizon check
only if required by the claim, after reset/RNG fixes, and new training-seed
confirmation before declaring the corrected protocol frozen. Main/ablation
YAMLs still contain 60k and five passes and must not be launched as though
they inherit this ten-pass gate. Comment-only BLOCKED markers are unenforced.

Secondary checkpoint audit: rebuilt from the **saved commstudy resolved
spec**, strictly loaded all nine entropy-.1 final group-policy checkpoints,
used a fresh CPU experiment per exploration mode, 32x100 steps, seed795630,
one thread. Python3.12.13/Torch2.13.0 CPU; other package versions match the
gate. This is not CUDA return reproduction. Only adversary action/loc/scale
leaves were measured. All 345,600 action scalars and all parameters emitted
by the distributions were finite. Deterministic saturation |a|>.999 was zero
everywhere. Maximum stochastic saturation was .026042% (.90 seed2); .95 had
.005208% seed0 and zero seeds1--2; .99 was zero all seeds. At .99 stochastic
mean scales were .111/.097/.071, so negative entropy here must not be called
the old exploding-scale/Tanh boundary failure. All scratch experiment trees
were removed, all imported checkpoints were read only.

Additional confirmed code findings:

- Same-input action/KL diagnostics currently compare different trajectories,
  and their first-leaf lookup is not group-safe. Implement recorded-input
  evaluation separately from paired full-episode reliance.
- Seed+reset leaves PCP wander_dir unchanged. Reproduced persistent .5 values
  in four worlds. At matched state/RNG, adding p1 dropout changes the next
  scripted prey action by max .05460807681083679. Isolate streams and reset
  all task state, including partial resets. Evaluation must preserve training
  RNG. Environment snapshots/noise schedules are needed for clean pairing.
- Explicit sender masks can defeat p1 channel swapping; severing needs a
  dominant intervention mask and no random draws.
- Evaluation-only p1 channel under no-grad DETERMINISTIC drops 12/12 senders;
  under no-grad RANDOM with the module still training it drops 0/12. Use an
  explicit phase, not exploration-type inference.
- `audit_run()` mixes both training groups; action diagnostics concatenate
  both groups. Batched audit/saliency rollouts ignore requested episodes.
- Confirmed wrong grouped override `model_config.params.comm_kwargs.num_heads=8`
  is accepted while actual PCP attention still has four heads. Shipped sweeps
  use the correct path, but strict general schemas/unused-key rejection are
  missing. Typed PCP TaskConfig is also missing.
- Critic inputs currently concatenate masked observations. Changing visibility
  also changes critic information. For an execution-information contrast,
  expose the same declared critic information across conditions, retaining the
  centralized MLP and keeping privileged state out of the actor.
- Fixed-random-K ordering is intentional but is not per-step random scheduling.
  Attention selects senders using global receiver/query/key scores; account
  for preview/scheduler information or label the scheduler idealized.
  Quantization does not change current float32 nominal bit accounting.
- MAPDN binding remains zero lines/unregistered. The existing sibling adapter
  is useful but requires real-feeder integration, time-block splits covering

## Research-grade roadmap deliverable 1 implemented (2026-09-05)

User authorized implementation of the thesis-wide research code roadmap, one
deliverable at a time, with PCP and MAPDN both in scope. Read
`../RESEARCH_CODE_ROADMAP.md`, `../RESEARCH_ASSESSMENT.md`, the overview and
current handoff, and inspected MAPDN's adapter read-only. This session completed
the first roadmap completion gate. No full-horizon scientific training, cluster
submission, commits, or historical artifact rewrites occurred.

Starting state: source HEAD `40a97a6`; `agents.md` already contained the user's
uncommitted assessment/roadmap history, and two .DS_Store files were untracked.
Those changes were preserved. MAPDN's three modified adapter/environment files
and untracked tests were pre-existing and remain untouched.

Implemented:

- Replaced cross-trajectory behavioural comparison with a separate
  `same_input_influence` API. It selects the measured group explicitly, retains
  declared observation/context (including supplied availability), removes stale
  output/generated-marker/other-group leaves, clones each input, and evaluates
  both conditions without env steps. Deterministic group joint-action L2 and
  pre-tanh diagonal-Gaussian KL are measured with finite-input/output checks.
  The standalone result records dataset source, keys/count/hash, and seed;
  batches use matched seed+index channel draws.
- Added `paired_episode_reliance`, explicit `EvaluationEpisode` bank and
  `EpisodeOutcome` records. Fresh one-world task instances guarantee the exact
  episode count independently of the existing test-env width. Records include
  IDs/seeds, initial-input SHA256, actual PCP exogenous schedule IDs/cursors,
  lengths, termination/truncation/completion, and returns. Incomplete episodes
  fail by default; mismatched, missing, duplicate/reordered IDs, initial states,
  or noise schedules cannot silently zip. Prefix mode is explicit and excluded
  from episode aggregation.
- Dominant channel intervention retains original modules and learned weights.
  Fully severed paths consume no random numbers and cannot be re-enabled by
  all-true replay masks. Partial masks intersect ordinary availability.
  Input validation still applies under severing; nested contexts, exceptions,
  private generators, cached sender masks, and communication stats restore.
- Dropout/Gaussian channels have independent private Torch streams, including
  independent sequential subchannel streams. Endpoint p=1 requires no draw.
  `channel_phase` makes evaluation explicit in both RANDOM and DETERMINISTIC
  action modes; PPO availability replay remains intact.
- PCP full/partial resets clear wander direction and noise cursors. Private
  per-world/prey noise blocks are indexed by episode and timestep and extend
  after 256 steps; unrelated policy/channel draws and other-world partial resets
  cannot alter an existing episode's disturbances.
- `IsolatedVmasEnv` wraps the task factory's simulator boundaries. A robust
  `RNGState` context preserves Python, NumPy, Torch CPU, initialized CUDA, and
  VMAS's shared class-level stream. Installed BenchMARL ALREADY protected
  CPU/Python/NumPy RNG on successful evaluation; its missing protections were
  VMAS shared state, CUDA, exceptions, and separate private channel streams.
  A thin `EvaluationIsolatedExperiment` wraps inherited evaluation only;
  collection, MAPPO/QMIX losses, and optimization remain upstream. Static eval
  repeats its own random namespace; nonstatic eval advances its own call index,
  independently of training draws.
- `actor_observation_keys` is declared in communication configs and validated
  against model input specs. Privileged/diagnostic/cross-group spec inputs and
  context overlap fail; unrelated runtime leaves cannot leak into actors.
  Stock MLP actor specs are selected explicitly by the PCP task.
- PCP has one stable 17-feature predator schema under global or radius-limited
  prey visibility. The root critic `state` contains absolute positions/velocities
  in world-agent order, landmark positions, and prey wander directions, with
  no reward/visibility/future-noise data. This 22-feature default vector feeds
  the unchanged centralized BenchMARL MLP through its supported state_spec path.
  Same physical states produce exactly the same critic inputs across visibility.
- Per-group actor/communication/critic parameter counts and runtime contracts
  are persisted in metadata. Default PCP predator actor/critic counts are
  19,332/19,585; scripted-prey actor/critic counts 156/193. Identity comm=0.
  Initial framework MLP/Identity actor outputs and counts remain exactly equal.
- `scripts/saliency.py` writes measurement schema2, explicit episode outcomes,
  runtime contract, and an atomically installed, content-addressed input dataset
  `saliency_inputs_<hash>.pt`. Legacy schema1 intervention files are preserved
  but warned/omitted by new aggregation, preventing old trajectory-based
  action/KL measurements from pooling with same-input influence.

A real TorchRL integration check exposed an additional substantive defect: raw

bootstrap. The task now removes raw VMAS's horizon and uses StepCounter(100),

calibration has different bootstrap semantics and remains historical evidence.

predator info leaves are checked then deduplicated. They cannot enter actors or
critics or advance RNG. expected_predator_reward supports all shaping/sharing
combinations; the candidate's return equals 10 times contact-pair transitions.
Simultaneous contact is descriptive geometry, not proof of communication or a
new terminal capture event.

### D5 bounded voltage-improvement confirmation complete — September 9, 21:21 EDT

The original fixed D5 confirmation exited **0** and wrote **CONFIRMED** at
**2026-09-10 01:21:48 UTC**, after **1615.41 s**. The scoped sleep assertion
also exited. Both seeds passed the declared voltage-reduction branch, complete
initial/final held-out banks, exact validation replay and evidence preservation.
The 16-episode banks each have 3,824 environment transitions, 126,192
bus-transitions and 22,944 action scalars. All eight full validation/test banks
were complete, solver-feasible and physically observed, with no solver failures
or curtailed PV; residual voltage violations remain. Here solver feasibility
means successful action and next-row power-flow solves, not voltage compliance.

Test voltage-violation magnitude decreased **93.3308%** for seed 30 and
**38.4848%** for seed 31. Seed 31's test values were
**0.0003830474566 → 0.0002356325966 pu**, and violated-bus counts were
**3,962 → 3,373 / 126,192**. Its reactive-power use nevertheless worsened
**0.261591 → 0.562727 MVAr/inverter**, line losses worsened
**0.077415 → 0.177465 MW**, and mean return worsened
**-10.489993 → -17.152317**. The same cost/return tradeoff appears in validation.
Seed 30 improved those descriptive means. The confirmed result is the
predefined voltage criterion on two fixed seeds and one feeder; it is not
confirmation of reward/efficiency optimization, complete safety, convergence,
general seed reliability or communication necessity. No post-outcome retuning,
replacement seed, retry or target revision occurred.

### D4 continuation stopped at historical archive binding — September 9, 21:31 EDT

Launch-time preflight passed with **345.49 GiB free**, both completed banks,
38 original unstarted rows and unchanged frozen package `69f202bf…`.
`coordinator_launch_time_preflight.json` SHA:
`9cae5eaff226b71384f85a79d28e1820070c819dbd386c1690b4b8480e0db581`.
Started supervisor session **48156**, supervisor PID **89263**, launched
process/group **89270**, at **01:31:03 UTC**; exact command, hashes and environment
are in `execution_launch.json`. It exited **2** at **01:31:18 UTC** after 14.79 s.
No process from that launch remains active, and **no new training row launched**.

Both recovered banks were copied byte-for-byte and passed strict evaluation
review in `execution/evaluations/`. The unchanged archive helper then rejected
both original rows with **`ValueError: Saved approval artifact differs`**.
Original metadata correctly binds the historical approval document; the derived
path-relocation manifest refers to a different-hash approval clone. The launch
review did not exercise and missed this recovery-only archival mismatch. The
frozen archiver's integrity check correctly stopped expansion. This is an
operational archive-binding failure; the original training and full held-out
banks remain valid and unchanged.

Preserve the entire `execution/` attempt, its two `archive_failure.json` records,
logs, copied/reviewed banks, supervisor receipts and unchanged coordinator v3.
The append-only observer was run against the terminal snapshot, recorded the
observed failure into this log and exited 0 as an observer (not a successful
study). Its receipt is `queue_observer_state.json`; future execution uses a
fresh state path.

The reviewed repair direction is to reuse only the **two original, independently
verified interim archives**, with exact member/source verification and verified
archive+receipt copies, plus a separate receipt binding the relocated manifest.
Original archive provenance and raw metadata will not be rewritten. All 38 new
rows retain the normal frozen archive helper. The helper and tests are being
implemented and will be checked against real temporary archive copies before a
fresh `execution_v2/` launch. No retraining, shortened bank or successful-until-
retry selection is involved; the 38 scientific attempts are still unstarted.

Separately, D5's independent raw-evidence review reproduced **CONFIRMED**:
all four full test banks (15,296 transitions total), both exact validation
replays, all five archives (380 members), native/managed checkpoint identities
and frozen inputs passed. Seed 31's 115.12% increase in reactive-power effort is
explicitly retained. Review:
`mapdn_confirmation_independent_review.json`, SHA
`bd8a8115bf0454f22fc033d8037a559bc44bf8816f0d69c409b103d2b804c067`.
The independent audit driver remains `/tmp/mapdn_confirmation_independent_review_20260909.py`
and should be retained in the final source/evidence archive.

D5's separate offline bootstrap also passed after preserving an initial
hash-omission failure for locally built wheels. A fresh dedicated environment
installed 67 exact hash-pinned dependencies; version/WHEEL-tag, Torch arithmetic,
local source/vendor origin, matching task contract and dependency consistency
checks passed. No main-site or `.deps` leakage, rollout, training or frozen
source mutation occurred. Proof:
`../.codex-worktrees/mapdn-development/results/mapdn_bootstrap_macos_arm64_cp312_20260909_attempt02/proof.json`
(SHA `5ed70ee416e45b9e3c5018b5ee78a9a4c3896f54b5d8211f212aafeb948d1192`).
Scientific workers and bootstrap verification have closed; remaining packaging
and documentation use only lightweight artifact work.

### Active queue observation and launch evidence preservation — September 9, 21:55 EDT

The append-only observer started at 01:51:35.958584 UTC as PID 91894
(supervisor 91887, tool session 66327). It polls the explicit v4 queue path every
15 seconds and stores separate `queue_observer_state_v2.json`, launch/log/exit
records. Its source SHA256 remains
`5c66696844d843b48579cc2e5acabfda82b0c59445c3dbc5989ffb6f4d7187a5`.
The first observed snapshot was committed successfully with no pending append.
Root uses advisory file locks for concurrent log appends.

Independent monitoring of the first new K0/K3 rows confirmed that both actual
launch metadata approval hashes match the relocated approval clone:
`062a5edaa0763ad3fab328b3fe253e39f54d470c7c387094c54165d3bdd5cb11`.
Their normal frozen archiver therefore does not encounter the historical
original-versus-relocated approval mismatch. Both rows were still training at
approximately 300k/294k frames at 01:53:13 UTC; these are progress observations,
not final health or scientific verdicts.

Root created `results/pcp_continuation_v4_launch_evidence_20260909.tar.gz` with
233 regular members, 1,796,713 bytes, SHA256
`3dc97f3197acf0cb9ee9987ed9988840964c69baf0635c1bb71054ca7bc235f2`.
The sidecar verification SHA256 is
`b72d659f5bea10d3653843176064321f13c2d284ebbcfb8b526fc6a0a553d278`.
Every member was read back and rehashed, all selected stable sources remained
unchanged during creation, and eight separate evidence-archive dependencies
were rehashed. The packet includes recovery code/tests, relocation packets,
failed v3 launch, v4 review/launch records, original-row reuse receipts, frozen
package source, current document snapshots, MAPDN summaries and figure evidence.

The packet deliberately describes a running allocation. It excludes changing
new raw runs and future outcomes. Main agents.md was captured under a shared
lock as `agents_at_v4_launch.md`; queue and supervisor snapshots are explicitly
named as snapshots. The producing script is retained as
`results/continuation_20260909/archive_v4_launch_evidence.py`. No original
research evidence was removed. Final D4 evidence still requires completion of
all allocated rows, full evaluations, strict reviews, archives and frozen analysis.

A separate read-only task is assessing the precise later integration delta for
D5 and how to retain strict D4 checkpoint reproducibility if main source changes
after the cohort closes. No scientific source is being edited during this run.

### First Broadcast pair fully closed; final reporting prepared — September 9, 22:15 EDT

K0 radius1/seed20 completed its fresh full bank, strict review and normal frozen
archive at 02:02:21.388733 UTC. K3 completed all six arms and the same final gates
at 02:10:35.835543 UTC. Only after each closure did the queue replace that slot
with the next original global-visibility row. Their archive SHA256 values are
`2b6ae60887a6bae8d1f506aedc243e12116111837e3ea4b58e6632f70be33a59`
and `29bd1e90c2f5006b6ab44c0963a3f8b2b4ee0572233d98fa08733d45de2f6b68`.
The first-pair monitor hit its agent usage limit after observing K0 closure;
this did not affect the coordinator, either worker or the append-only observer.
Root completed and persisted the independent review locally.

Root's stdlib review streamed all 290/289 archived payload files, checked gzip
trailers, exact member/size/mode inventories and current source bytes, and
verified all 226/225 raw-run files were covered. It rehashed 535 bound inputs
before/after, checked saved approval bindings, all episode identities and
complete 100-step banks, finite action summaries, reward and payload accounting,
and exact K0 live/severed metrics/action-health equality. K3's packet file hash
matches. Tensor/donor/native-content checks remain attributed to the hash-bound
frozen evaluator/strict review; root did not claim to rerun those checks.
Record: `first_broadcast_pair_independent_review.json`, SHA256
`15374de6deeccf165ddf9418947870fc018c30ccbcec2ef8c962118f4e1aea2d`.
Driver: `results/continuation_20260909/review_first_broadcast_pair.py`.
No policy rollouts, training or incomplete-cohort aggregation were performed.

The final-report schema checklist is
`results/continuation_20260909/d4_final_report_review.md`, SHA256
`08194131d100a710cedabea002979a2fd3e99a5f5d868aec9dbd6f0353ef47b9`.
It identifies all 14 analyzer outputs and 27 contrasts; intervention contrast
names must be selected together with their metric. Analyzer completeness is
`complete_for_review`, with approval still `not_issued`. It does not establish
a positive effect or a reached target. Exact controls, source/episode pairing,
archive readback, compute accounting and final figure inspection remain required.

Root added a separate reporting utility,
`scripts/summarize_pcp_budget_completion.py`, with contrast CSV, per-seed training
performance, compute accounting, Markdown and input/output provenance. It uses
only stdlib and requires all 40 rows and a terminal complete queue. It preserves
the frozen analyzer's intervals, K0=0 and lower-bound budget rule; no statistic
is recomputed. Nine synthetic stdlib tests and Ruff passed after three line-length
fixes. An independent review then identified additional manifest/row/archive
binding and concurrent-hash protections; that agent is adding those corrections
and an end-to-end synthetic export test. The reporting utility has not been run
on the incomplete study or treated as final until that review closes.

The exact post-D4 MAPDN integration plan is retained as
`mapdn_post_d4_integration_plan.json` (SHA256
`d85d16568faad67048eeb3b5b90a2af43211e4a8b7f7455551baad81793474e1`)
and Markdown (SHA256
`292b31e9d88e53bc46e7fd4aabf25d1af2855c420dad31e185da2eaa8c71b2f6`).
It identifies 95 candidate files, five replacements, no current base conflicts,
and the exact resulting D5 source fingerprint. Main integration must follow D4
closure and final evidence preservation, a verified saved D4 source/runtime
strict-reload proof, a reviewed isolated candidate and fresh root-bound D5
bootstrap/regression/validation-only engineering evidence. No package or runtime
changes are being made during the active cohort. Existing D3/D4/D5 evidence and
scientific test banks remain preserved.

Queue counts observed when appending: `{"completed": 5, "pending": 33, "running": 2}`. D4 remains incomplete.

## Research-direction interpretation — 2026-09-11T02:40:04.292381+00:00

The user asked whether the current results justify continuing the main research direction. Recommendation: continue a bounded validation phase within Simple Spread/PCP/MAPDN and fixed MAPPO; do not describe the current evidence as strong confirmation or launch the broad sweep yet. The controlled PCP result supplies no established positive Broadcast advantage, little restricted-visibility reliance, and a supplementary reliance interaction opposite to the predicted ordering. Substantial seed variation and unestablished convergence limit mechanism/necessity conclusions. MAPDN's Identity-only voltage learning is not evidence for communication utility. Preserve original predictions and negative outcomes; explanations developed after D4 require separately declared new evidence, not reclassification of the old test.

The next decision should follow the existing engineering/integration and held-out evaluator gates, a check of decision-relevant private information within the existing tasks, baseline-only training/validation budget selection, and one bounded paired MAPDN Identity/Broadcast comparison with live/severed domain outcomes. Evidence validity must be independent of obtaining a positive effect. A broader mechanism ranking needs a clear question and usable measurement after those checks; a stable negative result can support a narrower conclusion without implying general communication impossibility. No new experiment, source change, or runtime change was performed for this interpretation.

Primary methodological sources checked for this answer: Lowe et al., *On the Pitfalls of Measuring Emergent Communication* (AAMAS 2019), https://arxiv.org/abs/1903.05168, supporting caution about reward/message proxies; Agarwal et al., *Deep Reinforcement Learning at the Edge of the Statistical Precipice* (NeurIPS 2021), https://arxiv.org/abs/2108.13264, supporting uncertainty-aware interpretation of a small training-run allocation. These contextualize the evaluation approach and do not validate this project's hypothesis or establish novelty/publication readiness.
